CPU comparisons against the direct readers and the dual-backend real-data demo are the evidence; see docs/sciviz-workers.md and packages/sciviz/tests/workers{.test.js,-browser.mjs}. No speed, network throughput or zero-copy parsing claim is made. Reader feature/format limits remain the same.
### Batch 176 tensor fields
Claim: sciviz/tensor supplies symmetric tensor eigensystems on CPU/GPU, ellipsoid/superquadric glyphs, direction/FA colours, deterministic Euler/FACT fibres and transverse-eigenvalue hyperstreamlines. Diffusion fitting is explicitly the published ordinary log-linear least-squares model. Proof: tensor.test.js and tensor-real.test.js reconstruct analytic fields and compare all 8192 native OpenNeuro voxels with independent NumPy SVD least squares and eigh. tensor-browser.mjs checks the real-data render, GPU residual bounds and one instanced draw on WebGPU and compatibility WebGL2, plus symmetry, ownership, restoration and signed/physical-unit invariants. The fit is unconstrained and the paths are algorithmic, not a clinical or anatomical accuracy claim. Limits are explicit; no speed comparison is claimed.

## Scene-state shadow property identity (2026-11-10)
Shadow settings restored by save/scene-state retain their bound ordinary or null prototype, including when a snapshot owns __proto__. Proof: packages/save/tests/scene-state.test.js requires own data descriptors, exact detached recapture and prototype-preserving rollback after a later binding refuses its new property. Both new regressions fail on the previous Object.assign implementation. This claim covers shadow setting replacement; it makes no general security certification claim.

## Audio voice registration failure (2026-11-10)
AudioVoiceManager.add preserves the caller's existing playback/source and releases registration ownership when gain estimation rejects or throws. A stale disposed handle cannot release a later handle for the same sound. Proof: packages/audio/tests/voice-manager.test.js checks NaN, negative, infinite and throwing estimates, exact phase/source identity, other active voices and repeat registration. The three regressions fail before their repairs; valid-input offline/live waveform checks remain unchanged. This guarantee covers gain estimation and ownership, not arbitrary graph mutation inside caller callbacks.

## Optional Draco wide generic attributes (2026-11-10)

Claim: the owned optional decoder preserves nonzero UI8 component counts through
255 in its supported sequential, KD and predictor paths. Proof: native wide.rs
matches a frozen historical width17 public oracle and authored raw, integer,
quantized and signed/KD cases; the quad regression checks complete parallelogram,
multi and constrained predictions. draco-wide.test.js checks the shipped Wasm's
typed arrays and point indices at widths 1, 16, 17, 31, 64, 127 and 255. Existing
malformed and resource bounds remain. This does not establish default replacement,
historical predictor 3, malformed-text equivalence, RSS parity or speed parity.
## Direct MCP cancellation ownership (2026-11-10)
Concurrent tools/call requests cannot overwrite an active direct-server cancellation context by reusing its ID. Proof: packages/mcp/tests/server.test.js rejects a second dispatch, checks RPC.invalidRequest, cancels the original call and distinguishes numeric 7 from string 7 in a batch. Both regressions fail on the previous unchecked Map.set implementation. Stdio delegates to the same handler. No completed-ID session enforcement or new transport claim is made.

## Scientific picking and transfer initialization (2026-11-10)
CPU nearestPoint chooses the nearer projected point on its existing half-pixel screen-distance tie, using projectPoint's normalized depth convention. Proof: packages/sciviz/tests/pick.test.js checks an actual perspective camera in both input orders and an independent identity projection. bindTransfer restores its prior classifier and removes listeners if the initial render callback aborts or throws; dual failure reports both errors with code failed. Proof: packages/sciviz/tests/transfer.test.js; all new cases fail on the parent. GPU clip depth, the distance tie rule, default LUT dimensions and numerical acceptance bounds are unchanged.

## Mixer disposed insert ownership (2026-11-10)
A held disposed Bus releases its former optional stage/node references from its owned JavaScript containers. Proof: packages/mixer/tests/mixer.test.js traverses that ownership graph for bus and whole-mixer disposal, detects the retained nodes on the parent, and checks exact-once stage disposal and disconnected outputs. The probe excludes borrowed mixer/context owners and treats native node wrappers as leaves. No physical heap reclamation, GC timing, speed or DSP-quality claim is made.

## Audio listener registration disposal (2026-11-10)
Direct AudioListener disposal releases its own scene registration, making the name reusable without removing another scene's same-name listener. Proof: packages/audio/tests/audio.test.js checks replacement, stale-handle disposal, owner isolation, owned-context closure and lazy context preservation; both new regressions fail on the parent. packages/audio/tests/listener-disposal-browser.mjs checks the real engine on native WebGPU and WebGL2, including rendering after removal. No new core API or audio/rendering quality claim is introduced.

## Figure legend visibility (2026-11-10)
The default Figure's legend becomes non-rendered below two named layers and reappears with current labels when another is added. Proof: packages/sciviz/tests/legend.test.js fails on the old inline display override, and spec-browser.mjs reproduces the two-heatmap removal in a real browser on both backends, checking computed display, layout boxes and unchanged three-pixel row spacing. Numerical data, fixture layers and pixel thresholds are unchanged.

## Vector parser early cancellation (2026-11-10)
All five vector readers reject an already-aborted caller before converting input. Proof: packages/loaders/tests/geo.test.js checks Shapefile, MVT, GPX and KML with malformed bytes and wrong-shape binary input, and checks that GeoJSON does not invoke a caller's toJSON callback. All five cases fail on the parent. This is cancellation ordering, with the existing coded LoaderError contract; format output and validation are unchanged.

## Point octree load admission (2026-11-10)
OctreeCloud rejects concurrency settings that cannot represent a positive integer count of simultaneous loads. Proof: packages/points/tests/octree.test.js covers invalid numeric and wrong-type inputs, fails on the previous zero-limit acceptance, and observes real scheduled child loads at the default four and explicit one/eight limits. No hierarchy, point sampling, speed or pixel-quality claim changes.

## Behavior tree text option inference (2026-11-10)
General TreeTextOptions variables preserve possible subtree references in the inferred SubtreeDocument result; statically absent registrations retain TreeDocument. Proof: packages/behavior/tests/text-types.test.js compiles six inference cases and requires unsafe ordinary-tree assignment to reject. The original declaration fails with false type-equality checks and an unused expected-error diagnostic. Runtime syntax and validation are unchanged.
### Batch 176 molecules
Claim: optional sciviz/molecule displays PDB and mmCIF structures as ball-and-stick, space-filling or licorice using element-coloured analytic sphere/capped-cylinder impostors with curved depth, covalent-radius bonds and complete residue picking. Proof: loaders/tests/mmcif.test.js compares every 1CRN position, colour, radius and bond across both formats (327 atoms, 337 bonds, 46 residues), exercises CIF grammar and metadata, and checks explicit/symmetry connections. sciviz/tests/molecule.test.js checks representation defaults, analytic residue hits, input snapshots, validation and allocation ownership. molecule-browser.mjs checks both GPU backends, exact format and restore pixel identity within each backend, analytic intersections, one instanced draw and order-independent opaque depth. The real source is RCSB PDB 1CRN, CC0; documented fallback radii and bond heuristics are not a chemical bond-order or valence claim. No speed claim or optional ribbon implementation is asserted.


Stage glTF review M3: the one-call importer supports a basic-loader-only installation without loading pro or animation for plain geometry; JSON/GLB and missing-package regressions are in packages/stage/tests/gltf-routing.test.js and gltf-animation-missing.test.js. Full skin/morph/clip regressions remain in gltf-full.test.js. Research and limits: [loader selection](../stage-gltf-routing.md).

Draco encoder review L1: ranges near 1e-36 or beyond float32 range now produce a coded unsupported encode refusal rather than corrupt or undecodable output. Sequential and topology-selection paths are covered in packages/exporters/tests/draco-native.test.js, including exact bits-0 round trips through both decoders. Research and reproduction: [float range safety](../draco-float-ranges.md).

## Batch 137 clean-room encoder replacement

Review 5 corrects the encoder identity and external override description.
Loader recovery, CSP/SIMD error codes and allocation cleanup have deterministic
regressions in packages/exporters/tests/basis-loading.test.js. The default
requires Wasm SIMD; no scalar fallback is claimed. Reproduce these checks from
[Basis loader recovery](../exporter-basis-loading.md), which records the standards
and the simulated missing-SIMD coverage limit.

The optional exporters/ktx2 default encoder is Margo's Rust SIMD feature, without
the third-party encoder wrapper or binary. Existing public options and the custom
encoderPath compatibility route remain. All six measured mode/role cases fit the
old byte budgets and pass every mip PSNR/SSIM row on the unchanged full 1k CC0
Aerial Rocks 02 corpus: 66 PSNR and 42 valid Gaussian SSIM comparisons. This is a
corpus-specific matched-rate result, not universal quality or throughput parity.
Proof: results/batch-137-quality-r10.json; reproduction: reproduce.md. Huffman
literal coding changes no fitted byte; the data row now uses RDO 0.09375 instead
of the failing 0.25. No metric, tolerance, source, mip or assertion was weakened.
Throughput/load/memory comparisons remain scheduled for the quiet speed phase.
### Node glTF ordinary image sources (2026-11-10)

PNG and JPEG glTF images load without createImageBitmap using the existing optional image decoder. Evidence: node-images.test.js covers data URI, bufferView and fetched images, exact retained/uploaded bytes, colour-space selection, mip allocation, restoration, abort and invalid input. test-node-images.mjs uses the real Node binding and exact uniform-colour reference renders. test-pixel-images.mjs compares PNG bitmap/pixel uploads and restored models pixel for pixel on both backends. This claim covers these fixtures, not equality of all JPEG decoders or browser support for every optional image codec.
Entry byte evidence: the quality bundle method measures gltf-pro/model at 60,402 bytes gzip on the parent sources and 60,566 on the repaired sources (+164). This is a byte count, not a timing or speed claim.



### Integration J2: full 24-member C2 composition

34 sparse rows pass on native WebGPU and full WebGL2 compatibility at 1x and 4x
MSAA: 136 cases and 276/276 positive pairs, with independent four-state binary
coverage over the same adapter rows. Six RGBA probes, numeric reversed depth,
exact scene draw counts, target/MSAA/viewport/scissor restoration and simulated
XR sessions drawing real eye textures are asserted, with no collected errors.
[All numeric results](results/integration-j2-composition.json) retain backend,
row, sample count and explicit ANGLE Vulkan arguments for Windows tracer rows.
[Methods and clean-room sources](../integration-composition.md) bound the claim:
common core/post depth/stack/backend infrastructure is always present, only the
listed settings/probes are proven, XR delivery is simulated, and C1/C3-C10,
physical headsets, every option/pixel and higher-order interactions are outside
scope. The known ANGLE D3D11 tracer shader-link defect remains parked; no
assertion exception was used. No package source/API, core/stage or speed claim.

### J6 game integration

One README-composed game scene passes on native WebGPU and full WebGL2, with four exact RGBA probes, five scene draws unchanged after device destruction/recovery, and zero tracked device allocations after disposal. Evidence: results/integration-j6-game.json; reproduction and scope: ../integration-everything.md. No speed or universal-composition claim.

TAA deterministic reset (2026-11-10): exact named/global reset and replacement-device camera matrices are covered by packages/taa/test/view-lifecycle.test.js. The regression fails without the independent sample phase. Reproduce with npm run build:packages, then npm test --workspace @margoengine/taa. Sources and scope: docs/taa-reset.md. No speed or cross-backend byte-equality claim.

J6 scientific dashboard (2026-11-10): the eight optional packages in docs/integration-sciviz.md pass native/compatibility device-loss and disposal assertions with four exact probes, point/line counters and exact sum 20. Evidence: results/integration-j6-sciviz.json. Reproduce: npm run build:packages, then npm run test:integration:browser -- --scene=sciviz. One authored static scene on RTX A6000; no speed claim.

J6 public CAD-format viewer (2026-11-10): the public calls in docs/integration-engineering.md pass format/value assertions, exact native/compatibility restoration probes and zero tracked disposal. Evidence: results/integration-j6-cad.json. Reproduce: npm run build:packages, then npm run test:integration:browser -- --scene=engineering. Authored small mesh stand-ins on RTX A6000; no private CAD/BIM, large-file or speed claim.

### Notebook/share backend ownership
Sciviz notebook/share source imports and its manifest have no compatibility-layer dependency. Host adapters supply deferred, abortable setup. Evidence: unchanged webgl2-compat/test/isolation.test.js and sciviz/tests/backend-isolation.test.js; native/compat share and notebook browser controls. No speed or universal host claim. Reproduction and standards: ../sciviz-host-backends.md.

### J6 product viewer
On the recorded RTX A6000, the public product scene combines gltf-pro, materials, environment, staging, post, DOF, tonemap, TAA, selection, ViewCube and React. Four RGBA8 probes and four draws survive actual device replacement, match across native and both ANGLE paths and dispose with zero tracked ownership. The implicit-footprint emitter regression is independently reproduced with an oblique mip fixture. No full-image, HDR-bit, speed or other-GPU claim. Methods: ../integration-product.md; proof: results/integration-j6-product.json and results/integration-j6-product-diagnosis.json.

### Review 5 biased sampling

On RTX A6000, 20 oblique bias/offset/filter probes (1280 f32 words) match native WebGPU bits on ANGLE D3D11 and Vulkan. This is a fixture-specific result, not a universal filtering guarantee. Methods and reproduction: [bias sampling](../review5-compat-bias.md).
